## 1900 census vs Find a Grave (2026-11-02)
- FAG lists son Rudolph Hans Petersen (1893–1964). 1900 census lists son **Hans O.** b. May 1893. Possibly same person (Rudolph Hans / Hans O.); do not merge silently — ask Alex if known.
- Census surname **Peterson** (one s); later records often **Petersen**.
- 1900 household has five children; FAG also lists Ethel (1902), Elmer (1906), Olga (1911) — born after census, consistent.

## 2026-11-03 Hans dig
- Rudolph vs Hans O.: 1910 (S28) shows son "Rudolph H." age 16 in the slot where 1900 had "Hans O." b. May 1893. Treat as the SAME person (probable); the "O." initial is unexplained.
- Daughter Mary S. (1900, b. May 1898) = "Marie E." age 11 in 1910 (S28): same slot, but middle initial differs.
- Maren's birthplace: MN in 1900/1910 vs Denmark in 1920 (S29). Two records against one; MN preferred.
- Nels A. (b. 1891) is not in the 1910 household (age ~18, probably living elsewhere). He is still counted among the 7 living.
- Hans's year of naturalization in 1920 reads ~1897 (uncertain). Immigration 1884 is consistent across 1900 and 1910.

## 2026-11-03 — CANDIDATE Denmark origin for Hans Peter Petersen (P6) — UNVERIFIED, NOT family-confirmed
1. Copenhagen Police emigrant index, registered 16 Apr 1884: Hans Peter Petersen, last residence Bjernede, Sorø amt, age 20, dest. Springfield USA, contract 1015 Y. Index: https://www.aalborgstadsarkiv.dk/UA.asp?UA=UAProtokol
2. Bjernede Hovedministerialbog 1862–, FVD (births/baptisms), image 7 (epid=17223538): Hans Peder Pedersen b. 28 Feb 1864, bapt. 5 Jun 1864; parents TENTATIVELY **Hans Pedersen** & **Ane Rasmusdatter** (~28) per confirmation + re-read of this baptism; "2det Barn". The earlier reading "Niels Peder Pedersen / Ane Kirstine" is WITHDRAWN (misread) — see browser-pass-bjernede-corroboration.md. Viewer: https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/en/billedviser?epid=17223538
- Pedersen/Petersen is a normal spelling variant. No document yet links this Bjernede person to the Redwood Co MN Hans. Competing Tinglev/Gaardeby S21 candidate still NOT adopted.
- Birthplace in people.json stays "Denmark" (country only). Danish parents NOT added as people cards; recorded in notes only.
3. Confirmation 14 Apr 1878 (Bjernede, vol. 1858 K–1891 K, image 24): Hans Peter Petersen, parents Boelsmand Hans Petersen & Ane Rasmusdatter, b. 28 Feb 1864 / bapt. 5 Jun 1864 — matches baptism.
4. 1880 census Bjernede (image 5/71): Hans Peder Pedersen, 15, stepson of Hans Rasmussen (mother Ane remarried); low-confidence reads.
- These corroborate the Danish-side identity only. Still a CANDIDATE for Redwood Co MN Hans; birthplace stays Denmark (country only), parish unverified. Not family-confirmed. Source: browser-pass-bjernede-corroboration.md.
- Next free checks: 1870 Danish census Bjernede; original emigration protocol scan for contract 1015 Y; MN naturalization/death record parents.

## RESOLVED 2026-11-03 — Ethel Dohrmann maiden (S89 vs S90)
Was: Wesley Edward Dohrmann bio says married Ethel Gent (S89); her memorial says Ethel B. (Neubauer) Dohrmann (S90).
Resolution (Kin Dig F, S148/S149/S150): MNHS birth index (S148) Ethel Bertha Neubauer b. 1911-11-27 Brown Co, mother's maiden GENT, cert 1911-03110; MNHS death index (S149) Ethel B. Dohrmann d. 1994-06-07 Brown Co, b. 1911-11-27, mother's maiden GENT. Ethel's maiden = **Neubauer**; **Gent = her mother's maiden**. S89 bio misstated her maiden (likely used her mother's). Do not use Gent as Ethel's maiden; mention S89 wording only as a caution.
**2026-11-03 ~6:07 CT family context (Alex oral via Coordinator):** Ethel’s grandma Ghent/Gent raised her — upbringing note on P32 only; explains why obituaries and S89 say Ethel Gent/Ghent. Maiden stays Neubauer (S148/S149). **Not a surname reopen.** Dig 52 still Dohrmann→origin only.

## OPEN / CANDIDATE (2026-11-17, Dig R4d-3f S754) — Udvandrerarkivet index row for Petersen, Niels Jørgen, 14 May 1902, vs the unconfirmed original protocol
**CANDIDATE MED**, index only; original **NOT confirmed**. Both sides kept; no winner; not a HIT; not carded; not added to people.json. Pack: `digR/digR4d3f-s754-after-1901-wider-2026-11-17.md`. Gate: `digR/s754-gate-2026-11-17.md`. Index search: https://www.aalborgstadsarkiv.dk/UA.asp?UA=UAProtokol

**The index row.** **Petersen, Niels Jørgen**, Indirekte, stilling **Tyende (m/k land)**, fødested **Mulstrup**, sidste opholdssted **Johnstrup**, age **21**, destination **New York City, USA**, contract **2612 Clausen**, forevisningsdato **14/5 1902**.

**For treating this as our Niels Jørgen Pedersen** (b. 9 Jan 1881 at Mulstrup, Nordrup; last seen 1 Feb 1901 as an unmarried Tjenestekarl at Vandløse matr. 11, Stenmagle): the birthplace is Mulstrup; age 21 on 14 May 1902 fits a 9 Jan 1881 birth; the occupation Tyende fits the locked 1901 farm-servant placement. He could have moved after census night.

**Against identifying him as our man, and against treating the index as confirmed:**
- The surname is **Petersen**, not the locked **Pedersen**. Indexed searches for Niels Jørgen **Pedersen** in 1901–1920 returned none.
- Last residence **Johnstrup** is not Vandløse / Stenmagle. The place-name is itself ambiguous, with both sides kept and no winner picked: **Jonstrup** in Ugerløse sogn, Merløse herred, Holbæk amt, sits next to the parishes already walked, but this same index spells that Merløse place **Jonstrup**; **Johnstrup** / Neder Johnstrup in Saltum sogn, Hvetbo herred, Hjørring amt, is the spelling used for the other 1903 Johnstrup hit (Sørensen, Jørgen Jensen, born N. Saltum).
- The original protocol does **not** confirm the row at the two coordinates the index itself supplies. Københavns Politi, 3. Politiinspektorat, Register over udvandrere, Indirekte 1902–1903, AO epid **23522257**, bsid **725463**: contract **2612**, n178 id **97583172**, is **Pantz Janne** (best read), Tømrer, born Russland, age 37, destination Boston, about 2 May 1902; date **14 May 1902** on the P tab, n179 id **97583173**, shows a ditto continuation (Jens, contract 2998 Prahl) and **Petersen Johanna**, Skomager, Vejle / Børkop, 20, Tacoma, contract 3001 Prahl, in the 2998–3014 range, not 2612. Official crops: `digR/s754/s754-ao-725463-n178-97583172-kontrakt-2612-pantz-janne.jpg`; `digR/s754/s754-ao-725463-n179-97583173-1902-05-14-petersen-johanna.jpg`. https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/billedviser?epid=23522257#725463,97583172 https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/billedviser?epid=23522257#725463,97583173

**Next step, not a lock:** find the original line, for example in another 1902 volume or series, or by following the contract numbering by agent (Clausen / 2612). Until that line is read, this stays CANDIDATE MED and is not merged.

## OPEN / CANDIDATE (append 2026-11-17, Dig R4d-3g S765 / S768) — the 14 May 1902 protocol line is now confirmed; identity with our Niels Jørgen is CANDIDATE MED-HIGH, not a HIT, not locked
Evidence appended to the existing S754 CANDIDATE from R4d-3f. The original protocol line has now been found. Identification with our **Niels Jørgen Pedersen** (b. **9 Jan 1881** at Mulstrup, Nordrup, son of Johan Pedersen × Ane Marie Pedersen; S723 HIT MED confirmation; S727 HIT MED, last seen 1 Feb 1901 at Vandløse matr. 11, Stenmagle) stays **CANDIDATE MED-HIGH**. Both sides kept. Not a HIT. Not locked. Not carded. Lock and card are Alex’s call in Genealogist chat. Pack: `digR/digR4d3g-s764-1902-emigrant-2026-11-17.md`. Gate: `digR/s764-gate-2026-11-17.md`. Living-person guard applies to any later descendants.

**The confirmed departure (S765 HIT MED, single copy; Genealogist eye-read the crop).** Københavns Politi, 3. Politiinspektorat, Register over udvandrere, Indirekte 1901–1902, bsid **725462** n197 id **97582947**: **Niels Jørgen**, ditto surname best read **Petersen** from the heading **Petersen Johannes**, stilling **Tjener** / **Tyende**, fødested **Mulstrup**, sidste opholdssted **Johnstrup**, age **21**, destination **N.Y.**, contract **2612 Clausen**, forevisningsdato **14/5** [1902]. Official crops: `digR/s764/s764-ao-725462-n197-97582947-niels-jorgen-2612-14may1902.jpg`; `digR/s764/s764-ao-725462-n197-97582947-niels-jorgen-mulstrup-johnstrup.jpg`. https://arkivalieronline.rigsarkivet.dk/da/billedviser?epid=23522257#725462,97582947

The earlier disagreement with volume **725463** (S754 / S764) is explained, not erased: the Udvandrerarkivet index pointed only at the series epid, not at a volume or leaf; contract 2612 and 14 May 1902 on the P-tab in the 1902–1903 indirect volume are still Pantz Janne and Petersen Johanna. The matching line is in the adjoining 1901–1902 indirect volume.

**The arrival index (S768 HIT MED, index only; manifest not opened).** Ellis Island: **Niels Jorgen Pedersen**, 21, year of birth **1881**, single, last residence **Jonstrup, Denmark**, arrived **29 May 1902** at Ellis Island on the **Majestic** from **Liverpool**, film 280 frame 147 line 7, id **JFKP-FQS**. Image ark, not opened (Incapsula 403): https://www.familysearch.org/ark:/61903/3:1:3QS7-L9ND-G3XH

**For treating this emigrant as our Niels Jørgen:** the forenames; birthplace Mulstrup; age 21 on 14 May 1902 fitting a 9 Jan 1881 birth (21 years and four months); a servant’s occupation (Tjener / Tyende) as in the locked 1 Feb 1901 Tjenestekarl placement; and two independent records (the departure protocol and the arrival index). The 15-month gap after the 1901 census allows a move from Vandløse. The 15-day lag to a 29 May landing at New York, via Liverpool, fits an Indirekte contract. The arrival index uses **Pedersen** and **Jonstrup**, which agree with the locked surname and with the Ugerløse church-book spelling (S767, 158144 n003).

**Against:** no parents are named on the protocol or on the arrival index. The protocol surname, by ditto, is best read **Petersen**; the locked name and the arrival index are **Pedersen**. Last residence is **Johnstrup** on the protocol (and the S754 index), versus **Jonstrup** on the arrival index and in the Ugerløse 1892 baptism register. **Johnstrup** / Neder Johnstrup in Saltum sogn, Hvetbo herred, Hjørring amt, remains a possible reading of the protocol spelling. No winner is picked on the surname or the place.

**Read question, MED, not a conflict (S765 eye-read).** A word read as **Hansen** sits below Niels Jørgen’s name on n197, between his line and contract **2613** (Pahl Georg Mathias, Tømrer). It is unexplained; most likely it belongs to the next entry. It is not treated as a second surname and is not folded into the Petersen / Pedersen conflict.
